import { rankingService } from '../services/ranking.service.js';

/**
 * Controlador HTTP para Visualização das Tabelas de Classificação Competitiva (Leaderboards).
 * Suporta consulta de rankings Globais, Semanais, Mensais e por Nível de Dificuldade,
 * com suporte a alternância dinâmica de abas via Fetch API ou renderização EJS.
 */
export const rankingController = {
  /**
   * Renderiza a página de classificações com suporte a filtros e fixação da posição do jogador.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderRankings: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const period = req.query.period ? String(req.query.period).toUpperCase() : 'GLOBAL';
      const difficulty = req.query.difficulty ? String(req.query.difficulty).toUpperCase() : null;
      const page = parseInt(req.query.page, 10) || 1;

      // Validação defensiva dos parâmetros
      const validPeriods = ['GLOBAL', 'WEEKLY', 'MONTHLY'];
      const validDifficulties = ['INICIANTE', 'MEDIO', 'PROFISSIONAL', 'HOKAGE'];

      const resolvedPeriod = validPeriods.includes(period) ? period : 'GLOBAL';
      const resolvedDifficulty = validDifficulties.includes(difficulty) ? difficulty : null;

      // Carrega os dados de classificação e a posição do próprio utilizador (se autenticado)
      const [leaderboardData, userRankCard] = await Promise.all([
        rankingService.getLeaderboard({
          period: resolvedPeriod,
          difficulty: resolvedDifficulty,
          page,
          limit: 50,
        }),
        req.user
          ? rankingService.getUserRankCard(req.user.id, {
              period: resolvedPeriod,
              difficulty: resolvedDifficulty,
            })
          : Promise.resolve(null),
      ]);

      const isApiRequest =
        req.xhr ||
        req.path.startsWith('/api') ||
        (req.headers.accept && req.headers.accept.includes('application/json'));

      if (isApiRequest) {
        return res.status(200).json({
          success: true,
          period: resolvedPeriod,
          periodIdentifier: leaderboardData.periodIdentifier,
          difficulty: resolvedDifficulty,
          rankings: leaderboardData.rankings,
          userRankCard,
        });
      }

      res.render('pages/rankings/index', {
        title: 'Classificações Competitivas — Anime Quiz',
        layout: req.isAuthenticated ? 'layouts/dashboard' : 'layouts/main',
        rankings: leaderboardData.rankings,
        period: resolvedPeriod,
        periodIdentifier: leaderboardData.periodIdentifier,
        difficulty: resolvedDifficulty,
        currentPage: page,
        userRankCard,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Endpoint de API dedicado para alternância rápida de abas de classificação sem recarregamento de página.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  getLeaderboardJson: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const period = req.query.period ? String(req.query.period).toUpperCase() : 'GLOBAL';
      const difficulty = req.query.difficulty ? String(req.query.difficulty).toUpperCase() : null;
      const page = parseInt(req.query.page, 10) || 1;

      const leaderboardData = await rankingService.getLeaderboard({
        period,
        difficulty,
        page,
        limit: 50,
      });

      const userRankCard = req.user
        ? await rankingService.getUserRankCard(req.user.id, { period, difficulty })
        : null;

      res.status(200).json({
        success: true,
        period,
        periodIdentifier: leaderboardData.periodIdentifier,
        difficulty,
        rankings: leaderboardData.rankings,
        userRankCard,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },
};

export default rankingController;

import { roomService } from '../services/room.service.js';
import { roomRepository } from '../repositories/room.repository.js';
import { animeRepository } from '../repositories/anime.repository.js';
import { AppError } from '../middlewares/error.middleware.js';

/**
 * Controlador HTTP para Gestão de Salas Multiplayer, Lobbies e Arenas em Tempo Real.
 */
export const roomController = {
  /**
   * Renderiza a listagem de salas multiplayer públicas disponíveis e convites pendentes.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderList: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const page = parseInt(req.query.page, 10) || 1;
      const difficulty = req.query.difficulty || null;
      const categoryId = req.query.categoryId ? parseInt(req.query.categoryId, 10) : null;

      const [roomsResult, categories, pendingInvitations] = await Promise.all([
        roomRepository.findOpenRoomsPaginated({
          page,
          limit: 12,
          difficulty,
          categoryId,
        }),
        animeRepository.findAllCategories(true),
        roomRepository.findPendingInvitations(req.user.id),
      ]);

      res.render('pages/rooms/list', {
        title: 'Salas Multiplayer — Anime Quiz',
        layout: 'layouts/dashboard',
        rooms: roomsResult.rooms,
        totalRooms: roomsResult.total,
        totalPages: roomsResult.totalPages,
        currentPage: page,
        categories,
        pendingInvitations,
        selectedDifficulty: difficulty,
        selectedCategory: categoryId,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Renderiza a página de criação de uma nova sala multiplayer.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderCreate: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const categories = await animeRepository.findAllCategories(true);

      res.render('pages/rooms/create', {
        title: 'Criar Sala Multiplayer — Anime Quiz',
        layout: 'layouts/dashboard',
        categories,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Processa a criação de uma sala multiplayer.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  handleCreate: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const {
        name,
        isPrivate,
        password,
        maxPlayers,
        difficulty,
        categoryId,
        questionsCount,
        timePerQuestion,
      } = req.body;

      const room = await roomService.createRoom({
        hostUserId: req.user.id,
        name,
        isPrivate: Boolean(isPrivate),
        password: password || '',
        maxPlayers: parseInt(maxPlayers, 10) || 8,
        difficulty,
        categoryId: categoryId ? parseInt(categoryId, 10) : null,
        questionsCount: parseInt(questionsCount, 10) || 10,
        timePerQuestion: parseInt(timePerQuestion, 10) || 15,
      });

      const isApiRequest =
        req.xhr ||
        req.path.startsWith('/api') ||
        (req.headers.accept && req.headers.accept.includes('application/json'));

      if (isApiRequest) {
        return res.status(201).json({
          success: true,
          room,
          redirect: `/rooms/lobby/${room.code}`,
        });
      }

      res.redirect(`/rooms/lobby/${room.code}`);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Renderiza o lobby de espera da sala multiplayer antes do início da partida.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderLobby: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { code } = req.params;

      const room = await roomRepository.findByCode(code);
      if (!room) {
        throw new AppError('Sala não encontrada ou já encerrada.', 404, 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND');
      }

      // Se a partida já estiver a decorrer, redireciona para a arena activa
      if (room.status === 'IN_GAME') {
        return res.redirect(`/rooms/arena/${room.code}`);
      }

      // Garante que o utilizador esteja registado como jogador na sala
      await roomRepository.addPlayer(room.id, req.user.id, room.host_user_id === req.user.id);

      const players = await roomRepository.findPlayersByRoomId(room.id);
      const isHost = room.host_user_id === req.user.id;

      res.render('pages/rooms/lobby', {
        title: `Lobby: ${room.name} [${room.code}] — Anime Quiz`,
        layout: 'layouts/game',
        room,
        players,
        isHost,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Processa a entrada numa sala via código alfanumérico curto.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  handleJoinByCode: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { code, password } = req.body;

      const { room } = await roomService.joinRoomByCode({
        userId: req.user.id,
        code,
        password,
      });

      const isApiRequest =
        req.xhr ||
        req.path.startsWith('/api') ||
        (req.headers.accept && req.headers.accept.includes('application/json'));

      if (isApiRequest) {
        return res.status(200).json({
          success: true,
          redirect: `/rooms/lobby/${room.code}`,
          room,
        });
      }

      res.redirect(`/rooms/lobby/${room.code}`);
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Renderiza a Arena Multiplayer em tempo real (HUD de Jogo Sincronizado por WebSocket).
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderArena: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { code } = req.params;

      const room = await roomRepository.findByCode(code);
      if (!room) {
        throw new AppError('Sala não encontrada.', 404, 'ROOM_NOT_FOUND');
      }

      if (room.status !== 'IN_GAME') {
        return res.redirect(`/rooms/lobby/${room.code}`);
      }

      const players = await roomRepository.findPlayersByRoomId(room.id);
      const isHost = room.host_user_id === req.user.id;

      res.render('pages/rooms/arena', {
        title: `Arena: ${room.name} — Anime Quiz`,
        layout: 'layouts/game',
        room,
        players,
        isHost,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Envia um convite directo para outro jogador entrar na sala multiplayer.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  handleInvite: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { roomId, inviteeUserId } = req.body;

      const invitation = await roomRepository.createInvitation(
        roomId,
        req.user.id,
        inviteeUserId
      );

      res.status(201).json({
        success: true,
        message: 'Convite enviado com sucesso.',
        invitation,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },
};

export default roomController;

import { tournamentService } from '../services/tournament.service.js';
import { tournamentRepository } from '../repositories/tournament.repository.js';
import { paymentRepository } from '../repositories/payment.repository.js';
import { paymentService } from '../services/payment.service.js';
import { animeRepository } from '../repositories/anime.repository.js';
import { AppError } from '../middlewares/error.middleware.js';

/**
 * Controlador HTTP para Torneios Oficiais, Inscrições e Chaves Eliminatórias (Brackets).
 */
export const tournamentController = {
  /**
   * Renderiza a listagem de torneios abertos, em andamento e finalizados com filtros.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderList: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const page = parseInt(req.query.page, 10) || 1;
      const status = req.query.status || null;
      const difficulty = req.query.difficulty || null;
      const isPaid =
        req.query.isPaid === 'true' ? true : req.query.isPaid === 'false' ? false : null;

      const [tournamentsResult, categories] = await Promise.all([
        tournamentRepository.findPaginated({
          page,
          limit: 12,
          status,
          difficulty,
          isPaid,
        }),
        animeRepository.findAllCategories(true),
      ]);

      res.render('pages/tournaments/list', {
        title: 'Torneios Competitivos de Anime — Anime Quiz',
        layout: req.isAuthenticated ? 'layouts/dashboard' : 'layouts/main',
        tournaments: tournamentsResult.tournaments,
        totalTournaments: tournamentsResult.total,
        totalPages: tournamentsResult.totalPages,
        currentPage: page,
        categories,
        selectedStatus: status,
        selectedDifficulty: difficulty,
        selectedIsPaid: isPaid,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Renderiza os detalhes de um torneio específico, regras, premiação e estado da inscrição do jogador.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderDetails: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { slugOrId } = req.params;

      // Localiza por UUID ou por Slug
      const isUuid = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(slugOrId);
      const tournament = isUuid
        ? await tournamentRepository.findById(slugOrId)
        : await tournamentRepository.findBySlug(slugOrId);

      if (!tournament) {
        throw new AppError('Torneio não encontrado.', 404, 'TOURNAMENT_NOT_FOUND');
      }

      // Verifica se o jogador autenticado já possui inscrição e respectivo estado
      let userRegistration = null;
      if (req.user) {
        userRegistration = await tournamentRepository.findRegistration(tournament.id, req.user.id);
      }

      // Carrega a chave eliminatória (Rounds & Matches) caso já tenha sido gerada
      let rounds = [];
      if (['IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED'].includes(tournament.status)) {
        rounds = await tournamentRepository.findRoundsWithMatches(tournament.id);
      }

      res.render('pages/tournaments/details', {
        title: `${tournament.title} — Anime Quiz`,
        layout: req.isAuthenticated ? 'layouts/dashboard' : 'layouts/main',
        tournament,
        userRegistration,
        rounds,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Processa o pedido de inscrição do jogador no torneio.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  handleRegister: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { tournamentId, paymentMethod } = req.body;

      const result = await tournamentService.registerUser({
        tournamentId,
        userId: req.user.id,
        paymentMethod: paymentMethod || 'BANK_TRANSFER',
      });

      const isApiRequest =
        req.xhr ||
        req.path.startsWith('/api') ||
        (req.headers.accept && req.headers.accept.includes('application/json'));

      // Se for torneio pago, redireciona para a página de instrução de pagamento
      if (result.isPaid) {
        if (isApiRequest) {
          return res.status(200).json({
            success: true,
            isPaid: true,
            redirect: `/tournaments/${tournamentId}/payment`,
            payment: result.payment,
          });
        }
        return res.redirect(`/tournaments/${tournamentId}/payment`);
      }

      // Se for gratuito, inscrição confirmada
      if (isApiRequest) {
        return res.status(200).json({
          success: true,
          isPaid: false,
          redirect: `/tournaments/${tournamentId}`,
          registration: result.registration,
        });
      }

      req.session.flash = { success: 'Inscrição confirmada com sucesso! Boa sorte na arena!' };
      return req.session.save((err) => {
        if (err) return next(err);
        res.redirect(`/tournaments/${tournamentId}`);
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Renderiza a página de instrução de pagamento para torneios com taxa de inscrição em AOA.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderPayment: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { id: tournamentId } = req.params;

      const tournament = await tournamentRepository.findById(tournamentId);
      if (!tournament) {
        throw new AppError('Torneio não encontrado.', 404, 'TOURNAMENT_NOT_FOUND');
      }

      const registration = await tournamentRepository.findRegistration(tournamentId, req.user.id);
      if (!registration) {
        throw new AppError('Inscrição não localizada para este torneio.', 404, 'REGISTRATION_NOT_FOUND');
      }

      // Localiza a cobrança pendente gerada na inscrição
      const { rows: payments } = await paymentRepository.findAllPaginated({
        page: 1,
        limit: 1,
        search: req.user.username,
      });

      const payment = payments.find(
        (p) => p.tournament_id === tournamentId && p.user_id === req.user.id
      );

      const bankDetails = paymentService.getOfficialBankDetails();

      res.render('pages/tournaments/payment', {
        title: `Pagamento da Inscrição: ${tournament.title} — Anime Quiz`,
        layout: 'layouts/dashboard',
        tournament,
        registration,
        payment,
        bankDetails,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },

  /**
   * Renderiza a visualização isolada das chaves eliminatórias (Brackets) do torneio.
   * 
   * @param {import('express').Request} req
   * @param {import('express').Response} res
   * @param {import('express').NextFunction} next
   */
  renderBracket: async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { id: tournamentId } = req.params;

      const tournament = await tournamentRepository.findById(tournamentId);
      if (!tournament) {
        throw new AppError('Torneio não encontrado.', 404, 'TOURNAMENT_NOT_FOUND');
      }

      const rounds = await tournamentRepository.findRoundsWithMatches(tournamentId);

      res.render('pages/tournaments/bracket', {
        title: `Chave Eliminatória: ${tournament.title} — Anime Quiz`,
        layout: req.isAuthenticated ? 'layouts/dashboard' : 'layouts/main',
        tournament,
        rounds,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  },
};

export default tournamentController;

----- INICIO DO CODIGO -----

import pg from 'pg';
import { dbConfig } from '../config/database.js';

const { Pool } = pg;

/**
 * Instância única do pool de conexões com o PostgreSQL Neon.
 */
export const pool = new Pool(dbConfig);

// Listener para erros inesperados em clientes ociosos do pool
pool.on('error', (err) => {
  console.error('ERRO INESPERADO NO POOL POSTGRESQL:', err.message, err.stack);
});

/**
 * Executa uma consulta SQL parametrizada utilizando o pool de conexões.
 * 
 * @param {string} text - Comando SQL parametrizado ($1, $2, etc.)
 * @param {Array<any>} [params=[]] - Valores associados aos parâmetros
 * @returns {Promise<pg.QueryResult>}
 */
export const query = async (text, params = []) => {
  const start = Date.now();
  try {
    const res = await pool.query(text, params);
    const duration = Date.now() - start;
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development' && duration > 200) {
      console.warn(`[SLOW QUERY] (${duration}ms): ${text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()}`);
    }
    return res;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[DATABASE QUERY ERROR]:', {
      query: text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(),
      params,
      message: error.message,
    });
    throw error;
  }
};

/**
 * Obtém um cliente dedicado do pool para operações manuais ou multi-declarações.
 * O cliente DEVE ser liberado obrigatoriamente através de client.release().
 * 
 * @returns {Promise<pg.PoolClient>}
 */
export const getClient = async () => {
  const client = await pool.connect();
  return client;
};

/**
 * Executa uma operação transacional completa com isolamento e controlo de integridade.
 * Realiza BEGIN antes da execução e encarrega-se do COMMIT ou ROLLBACK automático em caso de erro,
 * garantindo sempre a libertação do cliente de volta para o pool.
 * 
 * @template T
 * @param {(client: pg.PoolClient) => Promise<T>} callback - Função contendo as instruções transacionais
 * @returns {Promise<T>}
 */
export const withTransaction = async (callback) => {
  const client = await pool.connect();
  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN');
    const result = await callback(client);
    await client.query('COMMIT');
    return result;
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK');
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error('[DATABASE ROLLBACK ERROR]:', rollbackError.message);
    }
    throw error;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
};

/**
 * Testa a conectividade com o banco de dados Neon no arranque do servidor.
 * 
 * @returns {Promise<boolean>}
 */
export const testConnection = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await pool.query('SELECT NOW() AS current_time, current_database() AS database_name');
    console.log(`[DATABASE] Conexão estabelecida com sucesso: ${res.rows[0].database_name} às ${res.rows[0].current_time}`);
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[DATABASE] Falha crítica ao conectar com o PostgreSQL Neon:', error.message);
    return false;
  }
};

/**
 * Encerra o pool de conexões de forma graciosa durante o encerramento do processo.
 * 
 * @returns {Promise<void>}
 */
export const closePool = async () => {
  try {
    await pool.end();
    console.log('[DATABASE] Pool de conexões PostgreSQL encerrado com sucesso.');
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[DATABASE] Erro ao encerrar o pool PostgreSQL:', error.message);
  }
};

export default {
  pool,
  query,
  getClient,
  withTransaction,
  testConnection,
  closePool,
};

FICHEIRO #52 : src\database\migrate.js

Caminho Completo : C:\Users\denyn\Downloads\ANIME-QUIZ\src\database\migrate.js

----- INICIO DO CODIGO -----

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { pool, closePool } from './connection.js';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const MIGRATIONS_DIR = path.join(__dirname, 'migrations');

/**
 * Cria a tabela de controlo de versionamento de migrações se não existir.
 * 
 * @param {import('pg').PoolClient} client
 */
const ensureMigrationsTable = async (client) => {
  const query = `
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
      id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
      version VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
      executed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    );
  `;
  await client.query(query);
};

/**
 * Executa todas as migrações SQL pendentes em ordem alfanumérica estrita.
 * Cada ficheiro é executado dentro da sua própria transação atómica (BEGIN/COMMIT).
 * Em caso de falha, ocorre ROLLBACK imediato da migração com erro e o processo é abortado.
 */
export const runMigrations = async () => {
  console.log('[MIGRATE] A verificar integridade e migrações pendentes...');
  const client = await pool.connect();

  try {
    // 1. Assegura a tabela de rastreio de migrações
    await ensureMigrationsTable(client);

    // 2. Consulta as migrações já executadas anteriormente
    const { rows: executedRows } = await client.query(
      'SELECT version FROM schema_migrations ORDER BY id ASC;'
    );
    const executedVersions = new Set(executedRows.map((row) => row.version));

    // 3. Lê todos os ficheiros .sql do directório migrations
    const files = await fs.readdir(MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    const sqlFiles = files
      .filter((file) => file.endsWith('.sql'))
      .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true }));

    if (sqlFiles.length === 0) {
      console.log('[MIGRATE] Nenhum ficheiro de migração encontrado.');
      return;
    }

    let appliedCount = 0;

    for (const file of sqlFiles) {
      if (executedVersions.has(file)) {
        continue;
      }

      console.log(`[MIGRATE] A aplicar migração: ${file}...`);
      const filePath = path.join(MIGRATIONS_DIR, file);
      const sqlContent = await fs.readFile(filePath, 'utf-8');

      const startTime = Date.now();

      // Executa cada ficheiro de migração dentro de uma transação isolada
      try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');
        await client.query(sqlContent);
        await client.query(
          'INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version) VALUES ($1);',
          [file]
        );
        await client.query('COMMIT');

        const duration = Date.now() - startTime;
        console.log(`[MIGRATE] ✓ Concluída com sucesso: ${file} (${duration}ms)`);
        appliedCount++;
      } catch (migrationError) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        console.error(`[MIGRATE CRITICAL] Falha ao executar migração [${file}]:`, migrationError.message);
        throw migrationError;
      }
    }

    if (appliedCount === 0) {
      console.log('[MIGRATE] A base de dados já se encontra actualizada. Nenhuma migração pendente.');
    } else {
      console.log(`[MIGRATE] Total de migrações aplicadas nesta execução: ${appliedCount}`);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[MIGRATE FATAL] Execução de migrações abortada:', error.message);
    process.exitCode = 1;
    throw error;
  } finally {
    client.release();
    // Encerra o pool caso o script tenha sido executado directamente pela linha de comandos
    if (process.argv[1] === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
      await closePool();
    }
  }
};

// Execução directa via `node src/database/migrate.js` ou `npm run db:migrate`
if (process.argv[1] === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  runMigrations()
    .then(() => {
      console.log('[MIGRATE] Processo de migração concluído.');
      process.exit(0);
    })
    .catch(() => {
      process.exit(1);
    });
}

export default runMigrations;

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 001: ROLES E PERMISSÕES (RBAC)
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Habilita extensões nativas essenciais para UUIDs e hashing
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

-- 2. Função reutilizável para actualização automática da coluna updated_at
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- 3. Tabela de Papéis (Roles) do Ecossistema
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS roles (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  is_system BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 4. Tabela de Permissões Granulares por Módulo
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS permissions (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  module VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 5. Tabela Associativa de Atribuição (Role <-> Permission)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS role_permissions (
  role_id INT NOT NULL REFERENCES roles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  permission_id INT NOT NULL REFERENCES permissions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (role_id, permission_id)
);

-- 6. Índices para Otimização de Consultas de Permissões
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_roles_name ON roles(name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_permissions_name ON permissions(name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_permissions_module ON permissions(module);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_role_permissions_role_id ON role_permissions(role_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_role_permissions_permission_id ON role_permissions(permission_id);

-- 7. Trigger para actualização de timestamp na tabela roles
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_roles_updated_at ON roles;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_roles_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON roles
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 002: UTILIZADORES, PERFIS E ATRIBUIÇÃO DE FUNÇÕES
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Tabela Principal de Credenciais e Autenticação (Users)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  username VARCHAR(30) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  email VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  is_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  verification_token VARCHAR(255),
  reset_password_token VARCHAR(255),
  reset_password_expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  last_login_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  CONSTRAINT chk_username_length CHECK (char_length(username) >= 3),
  CONSTRAINT chk_email_format CHECK (email ~* '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$')
);

-- 2. Tabela Associativa de Papéis do Utilizador (User <-> Role)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_roles (
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  role_id INT NOT NULL REFERENCES roles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, role_id)
);

-- 3. Tabela de Perfil Competitivo e Gamificação (User Profiles)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_profiles (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  display_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  avatar_url TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '/assets/images/placeholders/default-avatar.png',
  bio VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Gamer no ecossistema Anime Quiz.',
  xp BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (xp >= 0),
  level INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (level >= 1),
  total_score BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (total_score >= 0),
  matches_played INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (matches_played >= 0),
  matches_won INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (matches_won >= 0),
  matches_lost INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (matches_lost >= 0),
  rank_tier VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ESTUDANTE_ACADEMIA',
  country_code VARCHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'AO',
  phone_number VARCHAR(25),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 4. Índices para Otimização de Autenticação e Classificação
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_username ON users(LOWER(username));
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_email ON users(LOWER(email));
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_reset_password_token ON users(reset_password_token);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_roles_user_id ON user_roles(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_roles_role_id ON user_roles(role_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_profiles_user_id ON user_profiles(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_profiles_xp ON user_profiles(xp DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_profiles_level ON user_profiles(level DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_profiles_total_score ON user_profiles(total_score DESC);

-- 5. Triggers de Atualização Automática de Timestamp
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_users_updated_at ON users;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_users_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON users
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_user_profiles_updated_at ON user_profiles;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_user_profiles_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON user_profiles
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 003: TABELA DE SESSÕES PERSISTENTES (CONNECT-PG-SIMPLE)
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Criação da Tabela de Sessões HTTP / WebSocket
-- Esta tabela é gerida pela biblioteca connect-pg-simple em conjunto com o Express.
-- Armazena os cookies de sessão assinados dos utilizadores autenticados.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_sessions (
  sid VARCHAR NOT NULL COLLATE "default" PRIMARY KEY,
  sess JSON NOT NULL,
  expire TIMESTAMPTZ(6) NOT NULL
);

-- 2. Índice para Limpeza Eficiente de Sessões Expiradas
-- Permite que a rotina periódica de descarte (pruneSessionInterval)
-- execute DELETEs rápidos sem varrimento sequencial na tabela.
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IDX_user_sessions_expire" ON user_sessions(expire);

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 004: ANIMES, PERSONAGENS E CATEGORIAS DE QUIZ
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Tabela de Animes (Actua também como cache persistente da Jikan / MyAnimeList API)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS animes (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  mal_id INT UNIQUE,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  title_english VARCHAR(255),
  title_japanese VARCHAR(255),
  slug VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  synopsis TEXT,
  image_url TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '/assets/images/placeholders/anime-cover.png',
  banner_url TEXT,
  episodes INT DEFAULT 0,
  status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'Finished Airing',
  score NUMERIC(4, 2) DEFAULT 0.00,
  genres TEXT[] DEFAULT ARRAY[]::TEXT[],
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 2. Tabela de Personagens dos Universos de Anime
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anime_characters (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  anime_id INT NOT NULL REFERENCES animes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  mal_id INT,
  name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  role VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Main',
  image_url TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '/assets/images/placeholders/character.png',
  about TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 3. Tabela de Categorias Temáticas do Quiz
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quiz_categories (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  slug VARCHAR(120) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  icon_name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'sparkles',
  color_hex VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT '#00f2fe',
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 4. Índices para Pesquisas Rápidas e Matchmaking
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_animes_mal_id ON animes(mal_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_animes_slug ON animes(slug);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_animes_title ON animes(LOWER(title));
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_animes_is_active ON animes(is_active);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anime_characters_anime_id ON anime_characters(anime_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_anime_characters_name ON anime_characters(LOWER(name));

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_categories_slug ON quiz_categories(slug);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_categories_is_active ON quiz_categories(is_active);

-- 5. Triggers de Atualização Automática de Timestamp
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_animes_updated_at ON animes;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_animes_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON animes
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_anime_characters_updated_at ON anime_characters;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_anime_characters_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON anime_characters
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_quiz_categories_updated_at ON quiz_categories;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_quiz_categories_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON quiz_categories
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

----- FIM DO CODIGO -----

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 005: PERGUNTAS E RESPOSTAS DO QUIZ (TREINO E RANQUEADO)
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Criação do Tipo ENUM para as Quatro Dificuldades Oficiais
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'quiz_difficulty_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE quiz_difficulty_enum AS ENUM (
      'INICIANTE',
      'MEDIO',
      'PROFISSIONAL',
      'HOKAGE'
    );
  END IF;
END $$;

-- 2. Tabela de Perguntas do Quiz
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quiz_questions (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  category_id INT NOT NULL REFERENCES quiz_categories(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  anime_id INT REFERENCES animes(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  question_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  image_url TEXT,
  difficulty quiz_difficulty_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INICIANTE',
  explanation TEXT,
  time_limit_seconds INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 15 CHECK (time_limit_seconds >= 5 AND time_limit_seconds <= 60),
  base_points INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 100 CHECK (base_points > 0),
  base_xp INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 25 CHECK (base_xp > 0),
  is_published BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 3. Tabela de Alternativas / Respostas da Pergunta
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quiz_answers (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  question_id INT NOT NULL REFERENCES quiz_questions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  answer_text VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
  is_correct BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  display_order INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (display_order >= 1 AND display_order <= 4),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 4. Índices para Seleção Dinâmica, Matchmaking e Anti-Cheat
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_questions_category_id ON quiz_questions(category_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_questions_anime_id ON quiz_questions(anime_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_questions_difficulty ON quiz_questions(difficulty);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_questions_is_published ON quiz_questions(is_published);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_answers_question_id ON quiz_answers(question_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_answers_is_correct ON quiz_answers(question_id, is_correct);

-- 5. Trigger de Atualização Automática de Timestamp
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_quiz_questions_updated_at ON quiz_questions;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_quiz_questions_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON quiz_questions
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

----- FIM DO CODIGO -----

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 006: SESSÕES DE QUIZ, RESPOSTAS E RESULTADOS HISTÓRICOS
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Criação do Tipo ENUM para Modo de Quiz Individual
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'quiz_mode_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE quiz_mode_enum AS ENUM ('TREINO', 'RANQUEADO');
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'quiz_session_status_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE quiz_session_status_enum AS ENUM ('IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED', 'ABANDONED', 'TIMED_OUT');
  END IF;
END $$;

-- 2. Tabela de Sessões de Quiz Individual (Solo Run)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quiz_sessions (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  mode quiz_mode_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'TREINO',
  difficulty quiz_difficulty_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INICIANTE',
  category_id INT REFERENCES quiz_categories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  total_questions INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 10 CHECK (total_questions > 0),
  current_question_index INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  status quiz_session_status_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IN_PROGRESS',
  started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 3. Tabela de Respostas Submetidas em Cada Questão da Sessão
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quiz_session_answers (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  session_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES quiz_sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  question_id INT NOT NULL REFERENCES quiz_questions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  selected_answer_id INT REFERENCES quiz_answers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  is_correct BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  response_time_ms INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (response_time_ms >= 0),
  points_awarded INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (points_awarded >= 0),
  xp_awarded INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (xp_awarded >= 0),
  answered_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 4. Tabela de Resultados Consolidados da Sessão (Histórico e Auditoria)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quiz_results (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  session_id UUID UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES quiz_sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  correct_answers INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (correct_answers >= 0),
  incorrect_answers INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (incorrect_answers >= 0),
  unanswered INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (unanswered >= 0),
  accuracy_percentage NUMERIC(5, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00 CHECK (accuracy_percentage >= 0 AND accuracy_percentage <= 100),
  total_score INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (total_score >= 0),
  total_xp INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (total_xp >= 0),
  time_spent_seconds INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (time_spent_seconds >= 0),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 5. Índices de Desempenho e Auditoria
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_sessions_user_id ON quiz_sessions(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_sessions_status ON quiz_sessions(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_sessions_mode ON quiz_sessions(mode);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_session_answers_session_id ON quiz_session_answers(session_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_session_answers_question_id ON quiz_session_answers(question_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_results_user_id ON quiz_results(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_quiz_results_created_at ON quiz_results(created_at DESC);

----- FIM DO CODIGO -----

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 007: SALAS MULTIPLAYER, JOGADORES E CONVITES EM TEMPO REAL
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Criação do Tipo ENUM para o Ciclo de Vida da Sala Multiplayer
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'room_status_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE room_status_enum AS ENUM (
      'LOBBY',
      'STARTING',
      'IN_GAME',
      'FINISHED',
      'CANCELLED'
    );
  END IF;
END $$;

-- 2. Tabela de Salas Multiplayer (Lobbies e Arenas)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rooms (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code VARCHAR(8) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  host_user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  is_private BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  password_hash VARCHAR(255),
  max_players INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 8 CHECK (max_players >= 2 AND max_players <= 32),
  difficulty quiz_difficulty_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MEDIO',
  category_id INT REFERENCES quiz_categories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  questions_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 10 CHECK (questions_count >= 5 AND questions_count <= 50),
  time_per_question INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 15 CHECK (time_per_question >= 5 AND time_per_question <= 60),
  status room_status_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'LOBBY',
  current_question_index INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  ended_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 3. Tabela de Participantes da Sala (Room Players)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS room_players (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  room_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rooms(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  is_host BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  is_ready BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  score INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (score >= 0),
  correct_answers INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (correct_answers >= 0),
  rank_position INT,
  joined_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  left_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  CONSTRAINT uq_room_player UNIQUE (room_id, user_id)
);

-- 4. Tabela de Convites Diretos para Salas
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS room_invitations (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  room_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rooms(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  inviter_user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  invitee_user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING' CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'ACCEPTED', 'DECLINED', 'EXPIRED')),
  expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + INTERVAL '15 minutes'),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 5. Índices para Otimização de Consultas em Tempo Real e Listagem de Lobbies
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_rooms_code ON rooms(code);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_rooms_status ON rooms(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_rooms_is_private ON rooms(is_private);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_rooms_host_user_id ON rooms(host_user_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_room_players_room_id ON room_players(room_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_room_players_user_id ON room_players(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_room_players_score ON room_players(room_id, score DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_room_invitations_invitee ON room_invitations(invitee_user_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_room_invitations_room ON room_invitations(room_id);

-- 6. Trigger de Atualização Automática de Timestamp
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_rooms_updated_at ON rooms;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_rooms_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON rooms
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

----- FIM DO CODIGO -----

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 008: TORNEIOS, ELIMINATÓRIAS (BRACKETS) E INSCRIÇÕES
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Criação dos Tipos ENUM para Torneios e Partidas
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'tournament_status_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE tournament_status_enum AS ENUM (
      'DRAFT',
      'PUBLISHED',
      'REGISTRATION_OPEN',
      'REGISTRATION_CLOSED',
      'IN_PROGRESS',
      'COMPLETED',
      'CANCELLED'
    );
  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'tournament_registration_status_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE tournament_registration_status_enum AS ENUM (
      'PENDING_PAYMENT',
      'PROOF_SUBMITTED',
      'UNDER_REVIEW',
      'APPROVED',
      'REJECTED',
      'EXPIRED',
      'REFUNDED'
    );
  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'tournament_match_status_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE tournament_match_status_enum AS ENUM (
      'SCHEDULED',
      'IN_PROGRESS',
      'COMPLETED',
      'WALKOVER',
      'CANCELLED'
    );
  END IF;
END $$;

-- 2. Tabela Principal de Torneios
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournaments (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  title VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  slug VARCHAR(180) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  description TEXT NOT NULL,
  rules TEXT NOT NULL,
  banner_url TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '/assets/images/placeholders/tournament-banner.png',
  difficulty quiz_difficulty_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PROFISSIONAL',
  category_id INT REFERENCES quiz_categories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  max_participants INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 16 CHECK (max_participants IN (4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128)),
  is_paid BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  entry_fee NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00 CHECK (entry_fee >= 0),
  prize_pool NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00 CHECK (prize_pool >= 0),
  currency VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'AOA',
  status tournament_status_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
  registration_start_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  registration_end_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  tournament_start_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  tournament_end_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  champion_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  CONSTRAINT chk_tournament_dates CHECK (
    registration_start_at < registration_end_at AND
    registration_end_at <= tournament_start_at
  )
);

-- 3. Tabela de Inscrições de Jogadores no Torneio
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_registrations (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  tournament_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status tournament_registration_status_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING_PAYMENT',
  seed_number INT,
  registered_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  reviewed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  rejection_reason TEXT,
  CONSTRAINT uq_tournament_user UNIQUE (tournament_id, user_id)
);

-- 4. Tabela de Fases / Rondas Eliminatórias (Rounds)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_rounds (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  tournament_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  round_number INT NOT NULL,
  name VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL, -- Ex: 'Oitavas de Final', 'Quartos de Final', 'Meias-Finais', 'Grande Final'
  status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING' CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED')),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  CONSTRAINT uq_tournament_round UNIQUE (tournament_id, round_number)
);

-- 5. Tabela de Duelos / Confrontos Individuais (Matches)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_matches (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  tournament_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  round_id INT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournament_rounds(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  match_number INT NOT NULL,
  player1_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  player2_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  player1_score INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (player1_score >= 0),
  player2_score INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (player2_score >= 0),
  winner_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  status tournament_match_status_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SCHEDULED',
  scheduled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 6. Índices para Otimização de Chaves, Inscrições e Vagas Concorrentes
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_status ON tournaments(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_slug ON tournaments(slug);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_category_id ON tournaments(category_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_start_at ON tournaments(tournament_start_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_registrations_tournament ON tournament_registrations(tournament_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_registrations_user ON tournament_registrations(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_registrations_status ON tournament_registrations(tournament_id, status);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_rounds_tournament ON tournament_rounds(tournament_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_matches_round ON tournament_matches(round_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_matches_tournament ON tournament_matches(tournament_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_matches_players ON tournament_matches(player1_user_id, player2_user_id);

-- 7. Trigger de Atualização Automática de Timestamp
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_tournaments_updated_at ON tournaments;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_tournaments_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON tournaments
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

----- FIM DO CODIGO -----

Tamanho          : 4.83 KB

----- INICIO DO CODIGO -----

-- ==============================================================================
-- MIGRAÇÃO 009: PAGAMENTOS (AOA), COMPROVATIVOS BANCÁRIOS E TRANSAÇÕES
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Criação do Tipo ENUM para Métodos de Pagamento em Angola
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'payment_method_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE payment_method_enum AS ENUM (
      'BANK_TRANSFER',
      'MULTICAIXA_EXPRESS'
    );
  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'proof_status_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE proof_status_enum AS ENUM (
      'UNDER_REVIEW',
      'APPROVED',
      'REJECTED'
    );
  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'transaction_type_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE transaction_type_enum AS ENUM (
      'TOURNAMENT_ENTRY_FEE',
      'PRIZE_PAYOUT',
      'REFUND',
      'SYSTEM_ADJUSTMENT'
    );
  END IF;
END $$;

-- 2. Tabela de Cobranças e Pagamentos (Payments)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS payments (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  reference_code VARCHAR(32) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  tournament_id UUID REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  registration_id UUID REFERENCES tournament_registrations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
  currency VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'AOA',
  payment_method payment_method_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BANK_TRANSFER',
  status tournament_registration_status_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING_PAYMENT',
  expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT (CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + INTERVAL '24 hours'),
  paid_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 3. Tabela de Comprovativos Bancários Enviados (Payment Proofs)
-- Armazena os metadados e o hash criptográfico SHA-256 do ficheiro físico (guardado fora da pasta pública)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS payment_proofs (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
  original_filename VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  file_size_bytes INT NOT NULL CHECK (file_size_bytes > 0),
  mime_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  file_sha256 VARCHAR(64) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  payer_name VARCHAR(150),
  payer_account_or_phone VARCHAR(50),
  transfer_reference VARCHAR(100),
  transfer_date DATE,
  status proof_status_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNDER_REVIEW',
  submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  reviewed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  rejection_reason TEXT,
  admin_notes TEXT
);

-- 4. Tabela de Histórico Financeiro e Auditoria de Transações (Transactions)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS transactions (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  payment_id UUID REFERENCES payments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  type transaction_type_enum NOT NULL,
  amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL,
  currency VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'AOA',
  balance_after NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  description TEXT NOT NULL,
  performed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 5. Índices para Pesquisas, Auditoria e Integridade Financeira
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_reference_code ON payments(reference_code);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_user_id ON payments(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_tournament_id ON payments(tournament_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_registration_id ON payments(registration_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_status ON payments(status);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payment_proofs_payment_id ON payment_proofs(payment_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payment_proofs_user_id ON payment_proofs(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payment_proofs_status ON payment_proofs(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payment_proofs_sha256 ON payment_proofs(file_sha256);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_transactions_user_id ON transactions(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_transactions_payment_id ON transactions(payment_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_transactions_type ON transactions(type);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_transactions_created_at ON transactions(created_at DESC);

-- 6. Trigger de Atualização Automática de Timestamp
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_payments_updated_at ON payments;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_payments_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON payments
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'achievement_tier_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE achievement_tier_enum AS ENUM ('BRONZE', 'SILVER', 'GOLD', 'PLATINUM', 'DIAMOND');
  END IF;
END $$;

-- 2. Tabela de Classificações Competitivas Activas (Rankings)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rankings (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  period ranking_period_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'GLOBAL',
  period_identifier VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ALL_TIME', -- Ex: 'ALL_TIME', '2026-W44', '2026-11'
  difficulty quiz_difficulty_enum, -- NULL para classificação geral ou valor específico de dificuldade
  score BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (score >= 0),
  xp BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (xp >= 0),
  wins INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (wins >= 0),
  matches_played INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (matches_played >= 0),
  win_rate NUMERIC(5, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00 CHECK (win_rate >= 0 AND win_rate <= 100),
  current_position INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  previous_position INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  CONSTRAINT uq_user_ranking_period UNIQUE (user_id, period, period_identifier, difficulty)
);

-- 3. Tabela de Registos Históricos de Fim de Temporada (Ranking Snapshots)
-- Preserva pódios e pontuações consolidadas semanais/mensais para distribuição de recompensas
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ranking_snapshots (
  id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  period ranking_period_enum NOT NULL,
  period_identifier VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  final_position INT NOT NULL,
  score BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  xp BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  wins INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  rewards_granted JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  snapshotted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

  icon_url TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '/assets/icons/achievement-default.svg',
  badge_tier achievement_tier_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BRONZE',
  xp_reward INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 100 CHECK (xp_reward >= 0),
  criteria_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- Ex: 'WINS_COUNT', 'PERFECT_SCORE', 'TOURNAMENT_CHAMPION', 'HOKAGE_STREAK'
  criteria_threshold INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (criteria_threshold > 0),

);

    CREATE TYPE conversation_type_enum AS ENUM (
      'GLOBAL',
      'ROOM',
      'TOURNAMENT',
      'DIRECT'
    );
  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'notification_type_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE notification_type_enum AS ENUM (
      'SYSTEM',

      'SECURITY'
    );

-- 2. Tabela de Conversações / Canais de Chat (Conversations)

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),

  title VARCHAR(100),
  room_id UUID REFERENCES rooms(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  tournament_id UUID REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  role VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MEMBER' CHECK (role IN ('MEMBER', 'MODERATOR', 'ADMIN')),
  is_muted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  muted_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
  joined_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  last_read_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  CONSTRAINT uq_conversation_member UNIQUE (conversation_id, user_id)
);

-- 4. Tabela de Mensagens do Chat com Auditoria de Moderação

  sender_user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content VARCHAR(1000) NOT NULL,
  is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  deleted_by UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  deleted_reason VARCHAR(255),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 5. Tabela de Notificações do Utilizador (Alertas de Pagamento, Torneios e Conquistas)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notifications (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  message TEXT NOT NULL,
  type notification_type_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SYSTEM',
  link TEXT,
  is_read BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  read_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 6. Índices para Recuperação Ultra-Rápida de Mensagens e Notificações Pendentes
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conversations_type ON conversations(type);

-- 1. Criação dos Tipos ENUM para Denúncias e Relatórios
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'report_reason_enum') THEN
    CREATE TYPE report_reason_enum AS ENUM (
      'CHEATING_SUSPICION',
      'TOXICITY',
      'OFFENSIVE_CONTENT',
      'SPAM',
      'PAYMENT_FRAUD',
      'OTHER'
    );
  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_type WHERE typname = 'report_status_enum') THEN

      'RESOLVED',

-- 2. Tabela de Denúncias e Moderação da Comunidade (Reports)

  target_id VARCHAR(100),
  ip_address VARCHAR(45),
  user_agent TEXT,
  previous_data JSONB,
  new_data JSONB,
  notes TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

  description TEXT,
  is_public BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  updated_by UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

FICHEIRO #65 : src\database\seed.js
Tamanho          : 4.23 KB
Caminho Completo : C:\Users\denyn\Downloads\ANIME-QUIZ\src\database\seed.js

----- INICIO DO CODIGO -----

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { pool, closePool } from './connection.js';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const SEEDS_DIR = path.join(__dirname, 'seeds');

/**
 * Cria a tabela de controlo de versionamento de seeds se não existir.
 * 
 * @param {import('pg').PoolClient} client
 */
const ensureSeedsTable = async (client) => {
  const query = `
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_seeds (
      id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
      name VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
      executed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    );
  `;
  await client.query(query);
};

/**
 * Executa todos os ficheiros de povoamento inicial (seeds) ordenados alfanumericamente.
 * Cada seed é executado de forma transacional e idempotente, garantindo integridade.
 */
export const runSeeds = async () => {
  console.log('[SEED] A verificar ficheiros de povoamento de dados...');
  const client = await pool.connect();

  try {
    // 1. Assegura a tabela de controlo de execução de seeds
    await ensureSeedsTable(client);

    // 2. Consulta os seeds já aplicados
    const { rows: executedRows } = await client.query(
      'SELECT name FROM schema_seeds ORDER BY id ASC;'
    );
    const executedSeeds = new Set(executedRows.map((row) => row.name));

    // 3. Lê todos os ficheiros .js dentro de src/database/seeds/
    let files = [];
    try {
      files = await fs.readdir(SEEDS_DIR);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.code === 'ENOENT') {
        console.log('[SEED] Directório de seeds ainda não contém ficheiros.');
        return;
      }
      throw err;
    }

    const seedFiles = files
      .filter((file) => file.endsWith('.js'))
      .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true }));

    if (seedFiles.length === 0) {
      console.log('[SEED] Nenhum ficheiro de seed localizado.');
      return;
    }

    let appliedCount = 0;

    for (const file of seedFiles) {
      if (executedSeeds.has(file)) {
        continue;
      }

      console.log(`[SEED] A executar rotina de seed: ${file}...`);
      const filePath = path.join(SEEDS_DIR, file);
      const fileUrl = pathToFileURL(filePath).href;

      // Importação dinâmica do módulo de seed (ES Modules nativo)
      const seedModule = await import(fileUrl);
      const seedFn = seedModule.default || seedModule.runSeed;

      if (typeof seedFn !== 'function') {
        throw new Error(`O ficheiro de seed [${file}] deve exportar uma função default ou 'runSeed'.`);
      }

      const startTime = Date.now();

      // Execução transacional de cada seed
      try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');
        await seedFn(client);
        await client.query(
          'INSERT INTO schema_seeds (name) VALUES ($1);',
          [file]
        );
        await client.query('COMMIT');

        const duration = Date.now() - startTime;
        console.log(`[SEED] ✓ Seed concluído com sucesso: ${file} (${duration}ms)`);
        appliedCount++;
      } catch (seedError) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        console.error(`[SEED CRITICAL] Falha ao executar seed [${file}]:`, seedError.message);
        throw seedError;
      }
    }

    if (appliedCount === 0) {
      console.log('[SEED] A base de dados já possui todos os seeds obrigatórios aplicados.');
    } else {
      console.log(`[SEED] Total de rotinas de seed concluídas nesta execução: ${appliedCount}`);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[SEED FATAL] Processo de seed abortado:', error.message);
    process.exitCode = 1;
    throw error;
  } finally {
    client.release();
    if (process.argv[1] === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
      await closePool();
    }
  }
};

// Execução directa via `node src/database/seed.js` ou `npm run db:seed`
if (process.argv[1] === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  runSeeds()
    .then(() => {
      console.log('[SEED] Processo de povoamento inicial finalizado com sucesso.');
      process.exit(0);
    })
    .catch(() => {
      process.exit(1);
    });
}

export default runSeeds;

FICHEIRO #66 : src\database\seeds\001_roles_and_admin_seed.js
Tamanho          : 7.67 KB
Caminho Completo : C:\Users\denyn\Downloads\ANIME-QUIZ\src\database\seeds\001_roles_and_admin_seed.js

----- INICIO DO CODIGO -----

import bcrypt from 'bcrypt';
import { env } from '../../config/environment.js';

/**
 * Seed inicial: Funções (Roles), Permissões do Sistema, Utilizador Administrador Master e Definições Base.
 * 
 * @param {import('pg').PoolClient} client - Cliente transacional da conexão PostgreSQL Neon
 */
export const runSeed = async (client) => {
  console.log('[SEED:001] A semear papéis (roles) e permissões de sistema...');

  // 1. Definição dos Papéis Base
  const roles = [
    { name: 'ADMIN', description: 'Acesso total irrestrito ao sistema e painel de administração', is_system: true },
    { name: 'MODERATOR', description: 'Moderação de partidas, aprovação de pagamentos AOA e gestão de denúncias', is_system: true },
    { name: 'PLAYER', description: 'Jogador padrão com acesso a treino, ranqueado, salas e torneios', is_system: true },
  ];

  for (const role of roles) {
    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO roles (name, description, is_system)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3)
       ON CONFLICT (name) DO UPDATE SET
         description = EXCLUDED.description,
         is_system = EXCLUDED.is_system;`,
      [role.name, role.description, role.is_system]
    );
  }

  // 2. Definição das Permissões Granulares
  const permissions = [
    { name: 'manage_tournaments', module: 'tournaments', description: 'Criar, editar e gerir o ciclo de vida de torneios' },
    { name: 'view_tournaments', module: 'tournaments', description: 'Visualizar lista e estatísticas de torneios' },
    { name: 'review_payments', module: 'payments', description: 'Visualizar comprovativos bancários enviados' },
    { name: 'approve_payments', module: 'payments', description: 'Aprovar inscrições e pagamentos manuais em AOA' },
    { name: 'reject_payments', module: 'payments', description: 'Rejeitar comprovativos inválidos ou fraudulentos' },
    { name: 'manage_questions', module: 'quiz', description: 'Criar, aprovar e editar perguntas e respostas do quiz' },
    { name: 'manage_categories', module: 'quiz', description: 'Criar e editar categorias e animes' },
    { name: 'moderate_chat', module: 'chat', description: 'Apagar mensagens e silenciar jogadores no chat' },
    { name: 'mute_users', module: 'chat', description: 'Aplicar sanções de silêncio a jogadores tóxicos' },
    { name: 'view_audit_logs', module: 'admin', description: 'Aceder aos registos de auditoria e operações de administradores' },
    { name: 'manage_users', module: 'admin', description: 'Suspender, banir ou alterar privilégios de utilizadores' },
    { name: 'manage_settings', module: 'admin', description: 'Modificar parâmetros operacionais do sistema' },
  ];

  for (const perm of permissions) {
    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO permissions (name, module, description)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3)
       ON CONFLICT (name) DO UPDATE SET
         description = EXCLUDED.description,
         module = EXCLUDED.module;`,
      [perm.name, perm.module, perm.description]
    );
  }

  // 3. Associação de Permissões ao Administrador e Moderador
  const { rows: dbRoles } = await client.query('SELECT id, name FROM roles;');
  const { rows: dbPerms } = await client.query('SELECT id, name FROM permissions;');

  const adminRole = dbRoles.find((r) => r.name === 'ADMIN');
  const modRole = dbRoles.find((r) => r.name === 'MODERATOR');

  // ADMIN recebe todas as permissões do sistema
  if (adminRole) {
    for (const perm of dbPerms) {
      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO role_permissions (role_id, permission_id)
         VALUES ($1, $2)
         ON CONFLICT (role_id, permission_id) DO NOTHING;`,
        [adminRole.id, perm.id]
      );
    }
  }

  // MODERATOR recebe permissões operacionais de pagamentos, chat e moderação
  if (modRole) {
    const modPermNames = [
      'view_tournaments',
      'review_payments',
      'approve_payments',
      'reject_payments',
      'moderate_chat',
      'mute_users',
      'view_audit_logs',
    ];

    const targetPerms = dbPerms.filter((p) => modPermNames.includes(p.name));
    for (const perm of targetPerms) {
      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO role_permissions (role_id, permission_id)
         VALUES ($1, $2)
         ON CONFLICT (role_id, permission_id) DO NOTHING;`,
        [modRole.id, perm.id]
      );
    }
  }

  // 4. Criação da Conta do Administrador Master (Hokage)
  console.log('[SEED:001] A semear conta mestre do Administrador...');
  const saltRounds = 12;
  const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(env.ADMIN_SEED.password, saltRounds);

  const { rows: userRows } = await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO users (username, email, password_hash, is_active, is_verified)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, TRUE, TRUE)
     ON CONFLICT (username) DO UPDATE SET
       email = EXCLUDED.email,
       password_hash = EXCLUDED.password_hash,
       is_active = TRUE,
       is_verified = TRUE
     RETURNING id;`,
    [env.ADMIN_SEED.username, env.ADMIN_SEED.email, passwordHash]
  );

  const adminUserId = userRows[0].id;

  // Criação ou actualização do perfil competitivo do Administrador Master
  await client.query(
    `INSERT INTO user_profiles (user_id, display_name, bio, rank_tier, country_code)
     VALUES ($1, 'Hokage Master Admin', 'Administrador e guardião do ecossistema competitivo Anime Quiz.', 'HOKAGE', 'AO')
     ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE SET
       display_name = EXCLUDED.display_name,
       rank_tier = 'HOKAGE';`,
    [adminUserId]
  );

  // Atribuição do papel ADMIN ao utilizador
  if (adminRole) {
    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO user_roles (user_id, role_id)
       VALUES ($1, $2)
       ON CONFLICT (user_id, role_id) DO NOTHING;`,
      [adminUserId, adminRole.id]
    );
  }

  // 5. Definições do Sistema Padrão (System Settings)
  const initialSettings = [
    { key: 'platform_maintenance_mode', value: 'false', value_type: 'BOOLEAN', description: 'Activa o modo de manutenção global da aplicação', is_public: true },
    { key: 'registration_enabled', value: 'true', value_type: 'BOOLEAN', description: 'Permite novos registos de jogadores na plataforma', is_public: true },
    { key: 'bank_account_name', value: env.BANK.accountName, value_type: 'STRING', description: 'Nome do titular da conta bancária oficial', is_public: true },
    { key: 'bank_name', value: env.BANK.name, value_type: 'STRING', description: 'Nome da instituição bancária', is_public: true },
    { key: 'bank_iban', value: env.BANK.iban, value_type: 'STRING', description: 'IBAN oficial para transferências bancárias em Angola', is_public: true },
    { key: 'multicaixa_express_phone', value: env.BANK.multicaixaPhone, value_type: 'STRING', description: 'Número telefónico oficial do Multicaixa Express', is_public: true },
    { key: 'default_currency', value: env.BANK.currency, value_type: 'STRING', description: 'Moeda padrão de operação do ecossistema', is_public: true },
    { key: 'global_chat_enabled', value: 'true', value_type: 'BOOLEAN', description: 'Habilita o canal de chat global para todos os jogadores', is_public: true },
  ];

  for (const setting of initialSettings) {
    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO system_settings (key, value, value_type, description, is_public)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
       ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE SET
         value = EXCLUDED.value,
         value_type = EXCLUDED.value_type,
         description = EXCLUDED.description,
         is_public = EXCLUDED.is_public;`,
      [setting.key, setting.value, setting.value_type, setting.description, setting.is_public]
    );
  }

  console.log('[SEED:001] Concluído com sucesso.');
};

export default runSeed;
